## `AGO-0116-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Length Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Raw Cotton Grade & Fibre Length Testing
- **Intro:** Length Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Raw Cotton Grade & Fibre Length Testing — Pre-Start Calibration and Environmental Verification: Ambient temperature: 21°C ± 1°C. Relative humidity: 65% ± 2%. Micrometer reading on reference block: 20.00 mm ± 0.01 mm. Inst…
- **Key tolerance:** Ambient temperature: 21°C ± 1°C. Relative humidity: 65% ± 2%. Micrometer reading on reference block: 20.00 mm ± 0.01 mm. Instrument warm-up time: ≥ 15 minutes.
- **Standards:** `ISO 2403:2014`, `ISO 4912:1981`, `ASTM D1440-07(2019) Standard Test Method for Length and Length D`, `ANQ 0116:2021`
